pwc_se_ai_refit_count = {
    set_variable = { pwc_ai_refit_total = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwc_ai_refit_infantry = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwc_ai_refit_mobile = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwc_ai_refit_armor = 0 }
    every_country_division = {
        limit = {
            OR = {
                division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_artillery
                division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_heavy_tank
                division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_infantry
                division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_mechanized
                division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_motorized
                division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_tank
                division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_tank_destroyer
            }
        }
        PREV = {
            add_to_variable = { pwc_ai_refit_total = 1 }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_infantry
            }
            PREV = {
                add_to_variable = { pwc_ai_refit_infantry = 1 }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_motorized
                    division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_mechanized
                    division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_artillery
                }
            }
            PREV = {
                add_to_variable = { pwc_ai_refit_mobile = 1 }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_tank
                    division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_heavy_tank
                    division_has_majority_template = pwc_unit_tank_destroyer
                }
            }
            PREV = {
                add_to_variable = { pwc_ai_refit_armor = 1 }
            }
        }
    }
}
